我的标记：

Hot isostatic pressure treatment of 3D printed Ti6Al4V alters surface modifications and cellular response.

Medium AM Biomedical Materials pubmed Peripheral Background only in_vitro Exp. validation
Michael B Berger, Thomas W Jacobs, Barbara D Boyan, Zvi Schwartz Journal of biomedical materials research. Part B, Applied biomaterials 2020-05-01 10.1002/jbm.b.34474
单位: Department of Biomedical Engineering, College of Engineering, Virginia Commonwealth University, Richmond, Virginia.
通讯: Zvi Schwartz @ Department of Biomedical Engineering, College of Engineering, Virginia Commonwealth University, Richmond, Virginia.; Dep [推断]
相关性: 该论文涉及应力遮挡和骨整合，但缺少力学测试和疲劳分析，与用户关注的多孔结构力学性能匹配方向关联度较低。
边界: This paper does not include any mechanical testing (fatigue or compression) or FEA, focusing instead on surface characterization and cell biology, lacking the mechanical performance validation required for direct relevance to AM biomedical materials.
动机· 研究热等静压处理对3D打印Ti6Al4V植入物表面改性和细胞响应的影响。
方法· 制造简单多孔和骨小梁状多孔结构，进行HIP、喷砂和酸蚀处理，培养间充质干细胞。
结果· HIP处理改变表面形貌但不影响成骨分化，细胞对复杂多孔结构更敏感。
验证· 体外细胞培养实验验证。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To investigate how hot isostatic pressure treatment alters surface modifications and cellular response of 3D printed Ti6Al4V implants.
Method· Two porous constructs (simple and trabecular-like) were fabricated, subjected to HIP, grit-blasting, and acid-etching, and cultured with human mesenchymal stem cells.
Result· HIP treatment modified surface topography but did not affect osteogenic differentiation; cells showed greater differentiation on complex trabecular-like porosity.
Validation· In vitro cell culture experiments with MSCs.
hot isostatic pressure · 热等静压Ti6Al4V · Ti6Al4Vadditive manufacturing · 增材制造osseointegration · 骨整合surface roughness · 表面粗糙度mesenchymal stem cells · 间充质干细胞stress shielding · 应力遮挡porous architecture · 多孔结构implant surface treatment · 植入物表面处理osteogenic differentiation · 成骨分化
additive manufacturing Ti6Al4V hot isostatic pressing surface treatment osseointegration stem cells porous structure
标记：

Rational design, bio-functionalization and biological performance of hybrid additive manufactured titanium implants for orthopaedic applications: A review.

Medium AM Biomedical Materials pubmed Peripheral Background only none Review
Jun Li, Xiaolin Cui, Gary J Hooper, Khoon S Lim, Tim B F Woodfield Journal of the mechanical behavior of biomedical materials 2020-05-01 10.1016/j.jmbbm.2020.103671
单位: Christchurch Regenerative Medicine and Tissue Engineering (CReaTE) Group, Department of Orthopedic Surgery & Musculoskeletal Medicine, Centre for Bioengineering & Nanomedicine, University of Otago, Ch
通讯: Khoon S Lim @ Christchurch Regenerative Medicine and Tissue Engineering (CReaTE) Group, Department of Orthopedic Surgery & Musculoskel  |  Tim B F Woodfield @ Christchurch Regenerative Medicine and Tissue Engineering (CReaTE) Group, Department of Orthopedic Surgery & Musculoskel
相关性: 该综述总结了增材制造钛植入体的多孔结构设计及成骨整合因素，但缺乏原始实验数据。
边界: It is a review paper without original experimental validation, no LPBF fabrication or mechanical testing data presented.
动机· 总结增材制造钛植入体影响骨整合和感染抑制的关键因素
方法· 综述了设计、制造、生物功能化及临床评价的相关研究
结果· 强调增材制造通过材料优化、结构设计和表面改性实现长期功能
验证· 无原始验证，综合文献证据
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· This review summarizes the current status of additive manufacturing for bone-interfacing titanium implants, focusing on factors influencing osseointegration and infection inhibition.
Method· The literature on design, fabrication, bio-functionalization, and clinical evaluation of porous AM titanium implants is comprehensively reviewed.
Result· The review highlights the potential of AM to achieve patient-specific implants with optimized mechanical properties and biological performance through material development, implant design, and surface modification.
Validation· No original validation is provided; the paper synthesizes findings from multiple studies.
additive manufacturing · 增材制造titanium implant · 钛植入体osseointegration · 骨整合porous structure · 多孔结构surface modification · 表面改性bio-functionalization · 生物功能化Ti-6Al-4V · Ti-6Al-4V合金hybrid strategy · 混合策略
additive manufacturing titanium implants osseointegration porous structures surface modification review
标记：

Fatigue Strength of 316 L Stainless Steel Manufactured by Selective Laser Melting

Medium AM Biomedical Materials openalex Transferable Skim only mechanical_testing Exp. validation
Sepehr Hatami, Taoran Ma, Taina Vuoristo, Jens Bertilsson, Ola Lyckfeldt Journal of Materials Engineering and Performance 2020-05-01 10.1007/s11665-020-04859-x
单位: RISE IVF AB, Argongatan 30, 431 53, Mölndal, Sweden
通讯: Sepehr Hatami @ RISE IVF AB, Argongatan 30, 431 53, Mölndal, Sweden
相关性: 该论文研究了SLM制造的316L不锈钢的疲劳强度，但未涉及生物医学背景、多孔结构或应力遮挡。
边界: No porous structure, stress shielding, or biomedical context; focuses on manufacturing parameters rather than implant-specific performance.
动机· 评估SLM 316L不锈钢的疲劳强度与工艺参数关系
方法· 改变粉末层厚和机加工状态进行高周疲劳测试
结果· 机加工后疲劳强度显著提升，层厚影响较小
验证· 通过高周疲劳实验对比不同工艺条件
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· This study evaluates the fatigue strength of selective laser melted 316L stainless steel with varying layer thickness and post-machining conditions.
Method· High-cycle fatigue tests were conducted on specimens fabricated with 30 and 50 μm layer thickness in as-printed and machined conditions.
Result· Post-machining significantly improved fatigue strength due to reduced surface roughness and compressive residual stresses, while layer thickness had a minor negative effect.
Validation· Experimental fatigue testing was performed to compare the effects of layer thickness and post-machining on fatigue life.
selective laser melting (SLM) · 选区激光熔化fatigue strength · 疲劳强度layer thickness · 粉末层厚postmachining · 后机加工surface roughness · 表面粗糙度residual stress · 残余应力high cycle fatigue · 高周疲劳316L stainless steel · 316L不锈钢
SLM 316L stainless steel fatigue layer thickness postmachining
标记：

A peritectic phase refines the microstructure and enhances Zn implants

Medium AM Biomedical Materials openalex Peripheral Skim only mechanical_testing Exp. validation
Cijun Shuai, Zhi Dong, Chongxian He, Wenjing Yang, Shuping Peng et al. Journal of Materials Research and Technology 2020-05-01 10.1016/j.jmrt.2020.04.037
单位: Institute of Bioadditive Manufacturing, Jiangxi University of Science and Technology, Nanchang, 330013, China
通讯: Chongxian He @ Institute of Bioadditive Manufacturing, Jiangxi University of Science and Technology, Nanchang, 330013, China  |  Wenjing Yang @ Institute of Bioadditive Manufacturing, Jiangxi University of Science and Technology, Nanchang, 330013, China
相关性: 该论文涉及LPBF制备Zn-Cu合金植入物，但未研究多孔结构或应力遮挡，且力学测试仅包括静态压缩和拉伸，未涉及疲劳性能。
边界: No porous structure, no stress shielding or stiffness matching analysis, no fatigue testing, and no surface roughness or osseointegration evaluation.
动机· 开发可降解锌基骨植入材料，提高力学性能和抗菌性
方法· 通过LPBF制备Zn-Cu合金，利用包晶反应细化晶粒
结果· Zn-Cu合金屈服强度165 MPa，抗拉强度223 MPa，抗菌活性增强
验证· 力学测试（压缩、拉伸、硬度）和细胞/细菌实验
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To develop biodegradable Zn-based bone implants with improved mechanical properties and antibacterial activity.
Method· Zn-Cu alloy was fabricated by laser powder bed fusion, utilizing peritectic phase formation to refine grains and strengthen the material.
Result· The Zn-Cu alloy achieved compressive yield strength of 165 MPa, ultimate tensile strength of 223 MPa, and enhanced antibacterial activity.
Validation· Mechanical testing (compression, tension, hardness) and in vitro biocompatibility/antibacterial assays.
laser powder bed fusion · 激光粉末床熔融Zn-Cu alloy · 锌铜合金peritectic phase · 包晶相grain refinement · 晶粒细化antibacterial activity · 抗菌活性biodegradable implant · 可降解植入物
Zn-Cu alloy laser powder bed fusion grain refinement antibacterial biocompatibility
标记：

Self-optimizing grinding machines using Gaussian process models and constrained Bayesian optimization

Medium FEA & Surrogate Modelling openalex Transferable Save for project simulation_case UQ
Markus Maier, Alisa Rupenyan, Christian Bobst, Konrad Wegener The International Journal of Advanced Manufacturing Technology 2020-05-01 10.1007/s00170-020-05369-9
单位: inspire AG, Technoparkstrasse 1, 8005, Zurich, Switzerland
通讯: Markus Maier @ inspire AG, Technoparkstrasse 1, 8005, Zurich, Switzerland
相关性: 该论文将高斯过程模型与约束贝叶斯优化应用于磨削工艺自优化，其方法可迁移至植入物设计中的昂贵仿真采样与约束优化问题。
边界: This paper does not address biomedical implant design, FEA-based biomechanics, or uncertainty quantification beyond point predictions.
动机· 磨削工艺参数优化成本高，需高效自优化方法。
方法· 采用高斯过程模型构建代理，结合约束贝叶斯优化在线调整工艺参数。
结果· 实现了磨削过程的自适应优化，提高了加工效率与质量。
验证· 通过磨削仿真案例验证了方法的有效性。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· Grinding process parameter optimization is costly, requiring efficient self-optimization methods.
Method· Gaussian process models are used as surrogates, combined with constrained Bayesian optimization for online parameter tuning.
Result· Adaptive optimization of the grinding process is achieved, improving machining efficiency and quality.
Validation· The method is validated through grinding simulation case studies.
Gaussian process · 高斯过程constrained Bayesian optimization · 约束贝叶斯优化self-optimizing grinding · 自优化磨削surrogate modeling · 代理建模process parameter optimization · 工艺参数优化
Gaussian process constrained Bayesian optimization self-optimizing grinding surrogate modeling manufacturing
标记：

The use of density mapping in the analysis of thigh pain after total hip arthroplasty in patients with well-fixed tapered wedge stems

Medium Hip Implant & Femoral Stem openalex Peripheral Skim only clinical Exp. validation Patient-specific
Junya Yoshitani, Tamon Kabata, Yoshitomo Kajino, Takaaki Ohmori, Takuro Ueno et al. Journal of orthopaedic surgery 2020-05-01 10.1177/2309499020930306
单位: Department of Orthopaedic Surgery, Kanazawa University Graduate School of Medical Sciences, Kanazawa, Ishikawa, Japan
通讯: Junya Yoshitani @ Department of Orthopaedic Surgery, Kanazawa University Graduate School of Medical Sciences, Kanazawa, Ishikawa, Japan  |  Tamon Kabata @ Department of Orthopaedic Surgery, Kanazawa University Graduate School of Medical Sciences, Kanazawa, Ishikawa, Japan
相关性: 该论文探讨了全髋关节置换术后大腿疼痛与初始接触状态的关系，但未涉及应力遮挡或微动的定量力学分析。
边界: This paper does not quantify stress shielding, micromotion, or bone-implant interface mechanics; it uses density mapping from CT to assess contact area, not FE-based biomechanical modeling.
动机· 探究全髋关节置换术后大腿疼痛与假体初始接触状态的关系。
方法· 利用CT密度映射量化Gruen分区接触面积，进行回顾性病例对照研究。
结果· 大腿疼痛组在2区接触面积显著较小，接触面积与疼痛负相关。
验证· 基于209髋的临床数据，多变量逻辑回归分析。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To investigate the correlation between postoperative thigh pain and the initial contact state of cementless femoral stems in total hip arthroplasty.
Method· A retrospective case-control study using CT-based density mapping to quantify stem contact area in Gruen zones.
Result· Thigh pain was associated with significantly lower contact area in zone 2, and both contact area and canal flare index were negative predictors.
Validation· Clinical validation on 209 hips with multivariate logistic regression.
thigh pain · 大腿疼痛total hip arthroplasty · 全髋关节置换术density mapping · 密度映射Gruen zones · Gruen分区contact area · 接触面积tapered wedge stem · 锥形楔形柄canal flare index · 髓腔张开指数
hip implant thigh pain contact area Gruen zones tapered wedge stem

Multiparametric Analysis of Tissue Spheroids Fabricated from Different Types of Cells.

Low AI for Bioprinting pubmed Peripheral Background only in_vitro Exp. validation
Elizaveta V Koudan, Anna A Gryadunova, Pavel A Karalkin, Janetta V Korneva, Nina Y Meteleva et al. Biotechnology journal 2020-05-01 10.1002/biot.201900217
单位: Laboratory for Biotechnological Research 3D Bioprinting Solutions, Kashirskoe Highway, 68-2, Moscow, 115409, Russia.
通讯: Elena A Bulanova @ Laboratory for Biotechnological Research 3D Bioprinting Solutions, Kashirskoe Highway, 68-2, Moscow, 115409, Russia. [推断]
相关性: 该论文涉及组织球体的制造与表征，对生物打印有背景参考价值，但未使用AI/ML技术。
边界: No AI/ML integration into bioprinting workflow; purely experimental characterization without autonomous decision-making or predictive modeling.
动机· 需要可重复、可扩展且经济高效的组织球体制造与表征方法
方法· 采用非粘附技术结合多参数表征，系统分析不同细胞类型和组织球体属性
结果· 证明了球体直径、生长模式与细胞类型和初始接种浓度的强相关性
验证· 通过多种永生化细胞和原代细胞实验验证了方法的可重复性
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· The demand for reproducible, scalable, and cost-effective fabrication and versatile characterization of tissue spheroids is critical for 3D bioprinting and drug discovery.
Method· A straightforward non-adhesive technology combined with systematic multiparametric characterization was employed to produce and typify tissue spheroids with defined properties.
Result· Ultimate spheroid diameter, growth pattern, and mechanical properties strongly correlated with cell type and initial seeding concentration.
Validation· Experimental reproducibility was validated using both immortalized and primary cell lines across multiple batches.
tissue spheroids · 组织球体3D bioprinting · 3D生物打印biofabrication · 生物制造non-adhesive technology · 非粘附技术multiparametric characterization · 多参数表征cell viability · 细胞活力extracellular matrix · 细胞外基质drug discovery · 药物发现spheroid diameter · 球体直径cell derivation · 细胞来源
tissue spheroids biofabrication cell characterization non-adhesive technology spheroid fabrication
标记：

Bioengineering strategies for nephrologists: kidney was not built in a day.

Low AI for Bioprinting pubmed Peripheral Background only none Review
Anna Julie Peired, Benedetta Mazzinghi, Letizia De Chiara, Francesco Guzzi, Laura Lasagni et al. Expert opinion on biological therapy 2020-05-01 10.1080/14712598.2020.1709439
单位: Excellence Centre for Research, Transfer and High Education for the development of DE NOVO Therapies (DENOTHE), University of Florence, Florence, Italy.; Department of Experimental and Clinical Biomed
通讯: Elena Lazzeri @ Excellence Centre for Research, Transfer and High Education for the development of DE NOVO Therapies (DENOTHE), Universi [推断]
相关性: 该综述涵盖了包括3D生物打印在内的多种肾脏构建策略，但未涉及AI与生物打印的结合。
边界: This paper is a broad review of kidney bioengineering strategies including bioprinting, but it does not incorporate AI into any specific bioprinting workflow stage and lacks experimental validation of AI models.
动机· 解决终末期肾病的肾脏来源短缺问题
方法· 综述了异种移植、去细胞化支架、类器官及3D生物打印等多种策略
结果· 除异种移植外，多数方法离临床应用较远，但已可用于疾病建模和药物测试
验证· 无实验验证，为文献综述
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To address the shortage of donor kidneys for end-stage kidney disease by reviewing diverse bioengineering approaches.
Method· This review covers xenotransplantation, decellularized scaffolds, organoids, microfluidic devices, and 3D bioprinting for kidney fabrication.
Result· Most approaches remain preclinical but enable 3D disease modeling and drug testing, with xenotransplantation being closest to clinical translation.
Validation· No experimental validation is presented; this is a literature review.
kidney · 肾脏bioprinting · 生物打印organoid · 类器官decellularization · 去细胞化xenotransplantation · 异种移植microfluidic device · 微流控装置
kidney bioprinting organoid decellularization xenotransplantation

Design tools for patient specific and highly controlled melt electrowritten scaffolds.

Low AI for Bioprinting pubmed Peripheral Background only none Patient-specific
Naomi C Paxton, Matthew Lanaro, Arixin Bo, Nathan Crooks, Maureen T Ross et al. Journal of the mechanical behavior of biomedical materials 2020-05-01 10.1016/j.jmbbm.2020.103695
单位: Institute of Health and Biomedical Innovation, Queensland University of Technology, Brisbane, Australia; Science and Engineering Faculty, Queensland University of Technology, Brisbane, Australia.
通讯: Maria A Woodruff @ Institute of Health and Biomedical Innovation, Queensland University of Technology, Brisbane, Australia; Science and Eng
相关性: 该论文聚焦于熔融电写支架的设计工具，未涉及人工智能技术在生物打印中的应用。
边界: No AI/ML integration into bioprinting workflow; it is a software tool for pattern generation, not an AI-driven method.
动机· 熔融电写需要连续纤维，传统G代码不适用。
方法· 开发了自定义图案生成软件，支持多种支架几何设计。
结果· 实现了高度可控的几何和力学性能，能影响细胞行为。
验证· 未在论文中提供实验验证。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· Melt electrowriting requires continuous polymer flow, making conventional g-code unsuitable for scaffold fabrication.
Method· A suite of custom pattern generation software tools was developed to design scaffolds with crosshatch, gradient porosity, tubular, and patient-specific geometries.
Result· The approach enables highly controlled geometry and adaptable mechanical properties, with potential to influence biological properties.
Validation· No experimental validation is provided; the paper focuses on the design tools and their capabilities.
melt electrowriting · 熔融电写scaffold · 支架pattern generation · 图案生成patient-specific · 患者特异性extracellular matrix · 细胞外基质biofabrication · 生物制造g-code · G代码porosity · 孔隙率

3D Printed Hydrogel-Based Biosensors for Wearable Applications

标记：

Favorable modulation of osteoblast cellular activity on Zr-modified Co-Cr-Mo alloy: The significant impact of zirconium on cell-substrate interactions.

Low AM Biomedical Materials pubmed Peripheral Background only in_vitro Exp. validation
Na Gong, Ivan Montes, Krishna C Nune, R Devesh Kumar Misra, Kenta Yamanaka et al. Journal of biomedical materials research. Part B, Applied biomaterials 2020-05-01 10.1002/jbm.b.34499
单位: Biomaterials and Biomedical Research Laboratory, Department of Metallurgical, Materials and Biomedical Engineering, The University of Texas at El Paso, El Paso, Texas.
通讯: Akihiko Chiba @ Institute for Materials Research, Tohoku University, Sendai, Japan. [推断]
相关性: 该论文研究锆改性钴铬钼合金的细胞活性，但未涉及增材制造、多孔结构或疲劳性能，与AM生物医学方向关联较弱。
边界: No LPBF fabrication, no porous structure, no fatigue testing, no stress shielding analysis.
动机· 探究少量锆添加对钴铬钼合金细胞活性的调控作用。
方法· 通过细胞生物学与材料科学结合，比较含锆与不含锆合金的细胞行为。
结果· 含锆合金显著增强细胞粘附、铺展和细胞-基底相互作用。

英文摘要与术语

Method· Combined cellular biology and materials science to compare cell adhesion, spreading, and substrate interactions on Co-Cr-Mo and Co-Cr-Mo-Zr alloys.
Result· Zr-modified alloy showed enhanced cell spreading rate (190% vs. 120% per day) and higher cell density after 24 h incubation.
Validation· In vitro cell assays including vinculin, actin, and fibronectin staining.

Selective laser melting (SLM) of AISI 316L—impact of laser power, layer thickness, and hatch spacing on roughness, density, and microhardness at constant input energy density

Low AM Biomedical Materials openalex Peripheral Background only mechanical_testing Exp. validation
Sebastian Greco, Kevin Gutzeit, Hendrik Hotz, Benjamin Kirsch, Jan C. Aurich The International Journal of Advanced Manufacturing Technology 2020-05-01 10.1007/s00170-020-05510-8
单位: Institute for Manufacturing Technology and Production Systems, TU Kaiserslautern, Gottlieb-Daimler-Straße, 67663, Kaiserslautern, Germany
通讯: Sebastian Greco @ Institute for Manufacturing Technology and Production Systems, TU Kaiserslautern, Gottlieb-Daimler-Straße, 67663, Kaiser
相关性: 该论文研究SLM工艺参数对316L不锈钢表面粗糙度、密度和显微硬度的影响，未涉及生物医学植入物所需的应力屏蔽、疲劳或骨整合特性。
边界: This paper does not address porous structures, stress shielding, fatigue, surface roughness for osseointegration, or any biomedical context; it is a pure process-parameter study on AISI 316L.
动机· 研究恒定能量密度下激光功率、层厚和扫描间距对SLM工件性能的影响。
方法· 保持能量密度恒定，系统改变三个工艺参数并测量粗糙度、密度和显微硬度。
结果· 恒定能量密度不能保证一致性，密度和硬度可通过参数优化提升，且硬度与密度相关。
验证· 通过实验测量致密度和显微硬度验证，最高致密度达99.9%。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To investigate the influence of laser power, layer thickness, and hatch spacing on surface roughness, density, and microhardness in SLM of 316L under constant energy density.
Method· Three process parameters were systematically varied while adjusting scanning speed to maintain constant volumetric energy density; resulting part roughness, density, and microhardness were measured.
Result· Constant energy density does not guarantee consistent part properties; density and microhardness can be improved by selecting appropriate parameter combinations, and microhardness correlates with relative density.
Validation· Experimental characterization of density (up to 99.9%) and microhardness validated the findings.

验证· 通过流动性测试、密度测量和SLM成型实验验证粉末性能。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To investigate the spheroidization of Fe-based powders using a DC arc plasma torch and evaluate their suitability for selective laser melting.
Method· Fe and steel powders were treated in a plasma jet, and the effects on sphericity, flowability, and bulk density were analyzed.
Result· Spherical particle content up to 100% was achieved, with improved flowability (13–20 s) and increased bulk density (4.1–4.4 g/cm³); SLM samples reached a density of 7.86 g/cm³.
Validation· Powder properties were validated through flowability and density measurements, and SLM-fabricated samples were characterized for density.

动机· 改善生物医学植入物性能，减少应力遮挡。
方法· 采用定向能量沉积与层间铣削混合制造Ti-6Al-4V试样。
结果· 层间铣削使延展性翻倍，但抗拉强度降低，细胞生长不受影响。
验证· 通过拉伸测试和细胞粘附实验验证力学与生物性能。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To enhance biomedical implant performance by reducing stress shielding through hybrid additive manufacturing.
Method· Ti-6Al-4V samples were fabricated using directed energy deposition with interlayer milling to alter mechanical properties.
Result· Interlayer milling doubled ductility while reducing tensile strength, and cell adhesion remained unaffected.
Validation· Mechanical behavior was validated via tensile testing and cellular behavior via cell adhesion assays.

方法· 合成甲基丙烯酰化壳聚糖并研究浓度与取代度对打印性能的影响
结果· 高取代度CHI-MA可实现高分辨率、高保真度的3D水凝胶结构打印

边界: This paper does not use LPBF, does not evaluate stress shielding or stiffness matching, does not include mechanical testing or fatigue analysis, and does not characterize surface roughness or as-built morphology relevant to metal implant osseointegration.
动机· 评估3D打印生物陶瓷支架的孔结构对骨再生的影响。
方法· 在马来大动物模型中植入不同孔隙梯度支架，并用PCL外壳限制骨长入方向。
结果· 恒定孔隙结构比梯度结构促进更多新骨形成和支架降解。

英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To evaluate the effect of pore architecture of 3D printed bioceramic scaffolds on in vivo bone regeneration in a large animal model.
Method· Hydrogel-ceramic composite scaffolds with designed porosity gradients or constant pore distribution were implanted in equine critical-size defects, encased in a polycaprolactone shell to unidirectionally facilitate bone ingrowth.
Result· After 7 months, scaffolds with constant porosity showed significantly greater new bone volume and degradation than gradient scaffolds, suggesting anisotropic architectures modulate distant cell cross-talk.
Validation· In vivo validation was performed in an equine orthotopic model with histological analysis of bone ingrowth and scaffold degradation.

Method· Laser welding was applied to SLM-processed Ni-based alloy samples, followed by microstructural characterization and fatigue testing.

单位: Access e.V., Intzestr. 5, 52072 Aachen, Germany
通讯: J. Jakumeit @ Access e.V., Intzestr. 5, 52072 Aachen, Germany
相关性: 该论文研究IN718的激光粉末床熔融过程，未涉及生物医用材料、应力屏蔽或力学性能测试。

方法· 采用三相传热凝固模型，结合蒸发模型模拟单道熔池。
结果· 高速蒸发气流（达100 m/s）导致深锁孔和增加孔隙率。

Method· A three-phase melting and solidification simulation methodology extended with laser heating and metal evaporation models was applied to single track experiments.
Result· High laser power and slow speed produce high energy density, leading to deep keyhole formation and increased porosity, with gas velocities up to 100 m/s.
Validation· Simulation results were validated by comparing calculated melt pool depth with experimental measurements.

相关性: 该论文研究有机-溴化铅杂化材料的光致发光性能，与生物医学增材制造完全无关。
边界: This paper is about photoluminescent hybrid lead halides for lighting applications, with no LPBF fabrication, mechanical testing, porous structure, or biomedical context.

单位: Voronezh State Technical University, Moskovsky prospekt, 14, Voronezh, 394026, Russian Federation
通讯: И. Л. Батаронов @ Voronezh State Technical University, Moskovsky prospekt, 14, Voronezh, 394026, Russian Federation
相关性: 该论文研究多组分气体在多孔吸附剂中的传质模型，与生物医学增材制造中的力学性能、疲劳或骨整合无关。
边界: This paper does not cover LPBF fabrication, mechanical testing, stress shielding, fatigue, or osseointegration; it focuses on gas adsorption modeling in porous titanium.
动机· 建立强吸附极限下双组分气体通过多孔吸附剂的传质模型

Motivation· To develop a mass transfer model for two-component gas through a porous adsorbent under the competing adsorption framework in the strong adsorption limit.
Method· The system of two coupled equations is reduced to a self-similar form via the first integral, and two criterion relations governing the solution type are established.
Result· Self-similar expressions for absorbed mass distribution and total absorbed mass are derived, and barothermal relationships are computed for the air-titanium system.
Validation· Theoretical derivation with numerical calculations for the air-titanium system.

Validation· Statistical comparison of recorded data with survey responses.

Result· Students showed positive attitudes towards active learning strategies, which were perceived to improve language skills, but challenges such as unequal participation and large class sizes were noted.
Validation· Validation was based on survey responses and statistical analysis using SPSS.

Alessandro Petrini, Marco Mesiti, Max Schubach, Marco Frasca, Daniel Daniš et al. GigaScience 2020-05-01 10.1093/gigascience/giaa052

通讯: Giorgio Valentini @ CINI National Laboratory in Artificial Intelligence and Intelligent Systems - AIIS, Università di Roma , Via Ariosto 25,

边界: This paper addresses imbalanced classification in genomics, not surrogate modeling, FEA, or biomechanical design; it lacks any connection to expensive simulation sampling, constraint handling, or uncertainty quantification.
动机· 基因组中致病突变检测面临数据不平衡和大数据挑战
方法· 采用超集成、过采样和欠采样及并行计算技术处理不平衡大数据
结果· 在合成数据和真实基因组数据上达到最优结果，加速80倍

Result· The method achieves state-of-the-art performance on synthetic and real genomic datasets, with an 80-fold speedup over the sequential version.
Validation· Validation was performed using synthetic data and single-nucleotide variants associated with Mendelian diseases and GWAS hits.

动机· 探究项目式学习是否提高工程数学课程成绩。
方法· 对比有/无项目式学习的两组学生考试成绩，使用难度指数和相关性分析。

Validation· Statistical analysis of exam data from 764 students across two academic sessions.

Is a two-month healing period long enough to achieve osseointegration? A prospective clinical cohort study.

Exclude Hip Implant & Femoral Stem pubmed Peripheral Ignore clinical Exp. validation Patient-specific
M Cassetta, G Brandetti, F Altieri International journal of oral and maxillofacial surgery 2020-05-01 10.1016/j.ijom.2019.10.020
单位: Department of Oral and Maxillo-facial Sciences, "Sapienza" University of Rome, Rome, Italy. Electronic address: michele.cassetta@uniroma1.it.
通讯: M Cassetta @ Department of Oral and Maxillo-facial Sciences, "Sapienza" University of Rome, Rome, Italy. Electronic address: michele.
相关性: 该论文研究牙种植体的骨整合时间，与髋关节植入物及股骨柄的力学性能无关。
边界: This paper focuses on dental implant osseointegration in a clinical cohort, with no quantification of proximal femur stress/strain, no micromotion modeling, no FEA, and no comparison of intact vs implanted femur relevant to hip implants.
动机· 评估2个月愈合期对牙种植体骨整合是否足够。
方法· 通过共振频率分析测量种植体稳定性，比较不同时间点ISQ值。
结果· 2个月愈合期可达到二次稳定性，但6个月时稳定性显著更高。
验证· 临床前瞻性队列研究，142名患者268颗种植体。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To evaluate whether a 2-month healing period is sufficient to achieve osseointegration for dental implants.
Method· Implant stability was measured using resonance frequency analysis at insertion, 2 months, and 6 months post-surgery, with statistical comparison of ISQ values.
Result· Two-month healing allowed secondary stability, but ISQ significantly increased from 2 to 6 months, suggesting longer healing may be beneficial.
Validation· Prospective clinical cohort study involving 268 implants in 142 patients with 6-month follow-up and no dropouts.

单位: Southern Oregon Orthopedics, Medford, OR, USA; Department of Orthopaedic & Rehabilitation, Oregon Health & Science University, Portland, OR, USA. Electronic address: pjdenard@gmail.com.
通讯: Patrick J Denard @ Southern Oregon Orthopedics, Medford, OR, USA; Department of Orthopaedic & Rehabilitation, Oregon Health & Science Unive
相关性: 本研究关注肩关节置换术后的肱骨应力遮挡，与髋关节股骨柄的应力遮挡研究无直接关联。

方法· 多中心回顾性研究，比较93例压配和26例骨水泥固定组的影像学和功能结果。
结果· 两组功能结果无差异，但压配组近端应力遮挡更常见（68% vs 25%）。

Method· A multicenter retrospective review of 93 press-fit and 26 cemented standard-length humeral stems with identical geometry and coating.
Result· No significant differences in functional outcomes or loosening were found, but proximal lateral stress shielding was more frequent in the press-fit group (68% vs 25%).
Validation· Clinical and radiographic evaluation at minimum 2-year follow-up.

В. А. Щуров, L.V. Melnikova, Л. В. Мельникова DOAJ (DOAJ: Directory of Open Access Journals) 2020-05-01 link
单位: «Российский научный центр «Восстановительная травматология и ортопедия» имени академика Г. А. Илизарова». Курган, Россия
通讯: В. А. Щуров @ «Российский научный центр «Восстановительная травматология и ортопедия» имени академика Г. А. Илизарова». Курган, Россия
相关性: 该论文研究胫骨骨折的Ilizarov治疗，与髋关节植入物和股骨柄的应力遮挡、微动分析无关。
边界: This paper focuses on tibial fracture healing with Ilizarov external fixation, not on hip implant stress shielding, micromotion, or femoral stem biomechanics.
动机· 分析55岁以上胫骨骨折患者骨碎片微动与治疗时长。
方法· 通过应变片测量Ilizarov外固定架骨碎片微动，结合超声多普勒血流速度。
结果· 门诊治疗时微动增加，老年患者因运动模式温和而治疗时间缩短。

英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To analyze the dynamics of bone fragment micromotion and treatment duration in tibial fracture patients over 55 years old undergoing Ilizarov fixation.
Method· Micromotion of bone fragments was measured using strain gauges on Ilizarov wires under incremental axial loading, and blood flow velocity was assessed via Doppler ultrasound.
Result· Micromotion doubled during outpatient treatment, and elderly patients showed reduced treatment duration due to a more conservative motor regimen.
Validation· Clinical validation with 220 patients.

动机· 比较PFNA和DHS治疗骨质疏松性股骨转子间骨折的功能结局和翻修率
方法· 系统综述和meta分析，纳入9项研究共2251例患者
结果· 两种术式在1年Harris髋关节评分和翻修率上无显著差异
验证· 基于已发表文献的统计合并，无原始实验验证
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To compare functional outcomes and revision rates of PFNA versus DHS for osteoporotic intertrochanteric femur fractures.
Method· A systematic review and meta-analysis of 9 studies (2,251 patients) using random-effects models for continuous outcomes.
Result· No significant differences were found in Harris Hip Score at 12 months or revision rates between PFNA and DHS.
Validation· Statistical synthesis of published clinical data without experimental or biomechanical validation.

Implant Material As A Modifiable Risk Factor For Infection In THA: A Literature And Registry Review

Exclude Hip Implant & Femoral Stem openalex Peripheral Ignore none Review
Matthias Zimmermann, A. Leto, Alessandro Alan Porporati, H. Mannel Orthopaedic Journal of Sports Medicine 2020-05-01 10.1177/2325967120s00114
单位: CeramTec GmbH, CeramTec-Platz 1-9, 73207 Plochingen, Germany
通讯: Matthias Zimmermann @ CeramTec GmbH, CeramTec-Platz 1-9, 73207 Plochingen, Germany
相关性: 该论文讨论全髋关节置换术后感染风险与植入物材料的关系，不涉及股骨柄应力遮挡或微动分析。
边界: This paper focuses on infection risk and bearing materials, not on stress shielding, micromotion, or contact mechanics of femoral stems.
动机· 探讨全髋关节置换术后感染与植入物材料的关系
方法· 分析三大关节登记数据库及文献综述
结果· 金属轴承增加感染风险，陶瓷轴承降低感染风险
验证· 基于登记数据和文献回顾，无实验验证
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To examine the impact of bearing materials on periprosthetic joint infection incidence in total hip arthroplasty.
Method· Analysis of data from national joint registries (NJR, AOA, NZ) and literature review.
Result· Metal bearings are an independent risk factor for infection, while ceramic bearings are associated with lower revision risk for infection.
Validation· Registry data analysis and literature review, no experimental validation.

Incidence of epidural spread after Chayen’s approach to lumbar plexus block: a retrospective study

Exclude Hip Implant & Femoral Stem openalex Peripheral Ignore clinical
Elisabetta Rosanò, Diego Tavoletti, G. Luccarelli, Elisabetta Cerutti, Luca Pecora Brazilian Journal of Anesthesiology (English Edition) 2020-05-01 10.1016/j.bjane.2020.04.016
单位: Ospedali Riuniti, Marche Polytechnic University, Department of Emergency, Clinic of Anesthesia and Intensive Care Unit, Ancona, Italy
通讯: Elisabetta Rosanò @ Ospedali Riuniti, Marche Polytechnic University, Department of Emergency, Clinic of Anesthesia and Intensive Care Unit,
相关性: 该论文研究腰椎丛阻滞的麻醉技术，与髋关节植入物应力遮挡、微动或有限元分析无关。
边界: This paper is a clinical anesthesia study on lumbar plexus block for hip arthroplasty; it does not quantify stress shielding, micromotion, or any biomechanical modeling of the femoral stem.
动机· 评估Chayen入路腰椎丛阻滞的硬膜外扩散发生率
方法· 回顾性分析700例全髋置换患者的麻醉记录
结果· 硬膜外扩散率仅0.14%，显著低于其他入路
验证· 临床回顾性数据分析
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To evaluate the incidence of epidural spread using Chayen's approach for lumbar plexus block in hip arthroplasty.
Method· A retrospective review of 700 patients who received lumbar plexus block via Chayen's technique during total hip arthroplasty.
Result· Epidural spread occurred in only 0.14% of patients, significantly lower than reported rates for other approaches.
Validation· Clinical retrospective data analysis.
